# Stage 1 — making the engine execute (running notes)

## RECON (STAGE 3 — **op-0x24 WINDSHIELD FRONTIER: the op-0x0c "climb" is DISPROVEN as frame advancement — it is a SPIN ARTIFACT of a Ghidra word/byte mistype in `FUN_0000_22dd`; op 0x24 is never posted because the per-vehicle VIEWPORT method (`6f1f`/`73b9` & siblings) is never dispatched; both blockers trace to the ABSENT extender flight-model. NO engine/ext/shim change — pristine unchanged (`61453e42`/`0051cb56`/`75c6d726`); `bash tools/verify.sh both` = 19/19, native↔wasm bit-identical.**)

**Scope/method:** read-only recon on the genuine `BATTLES(160,100)->OK(205,128)->ACCEPT(40,186)` AZER1 run (write-isolated scratch datadir), via env-gated port instrumentation (op histogram, a d548/cursor probe, a `fist_icall_near` distinct-offset tracer, gdb breakpoints on the viewport methods) + `objdump` of `re_out/fist_dat_image.bin`. A THROWAWAY byte-fix of `22dd` (in `build/` only, reverted) was used to prove causality; nothing landed.

**FINDING 1 — op 0x24 is NOT posted; `FUN_0000_df0e` is never reached (op24=0 across a 36k-op ACCEPT run).** `df0e` posts op 0x24 (`if((param_3[0xd]&0x10)==0){ aa2c+0x3f2=param_2; aa10=0x24; e339(); }`, `fist.c:33199`). Its five callers are the per-vehicle inner viewport methods `6f1f`/`795c`/`8390`/`8cbf`/`93f3` (seg-0 thunks `73b9`/`77dc`/`7f44`/`8af2`/`61b0`/`73e7`). **gdb-proven:** on the ACCEPT run `FUN_1000_67e3` (the viewport-render init: `d548=1; d549=0x1e; d552=0x7aa4`) IS reached, and the EXTENDER drives `byte[DGROUP:0x1548]` (d548) to **0x81** (bit7 set + low7==1 = exactly the `df0e` gate), but NONE of the viewport methods/thunks ever fire (`FIST_NEARTRACE` shows 127 distinct near-dispatch offsets, none of them `73b9/77dc/7f44/8af2`). So the `df0e` gate is *satisfiable* (d548 reaches 0x81) — the viewport method that would test it is simply never dispatched.

**FINDING 2 — the viewport method's DISPATCH MECHANISM (image-verified).** The seg-0 viewport thunks live in a per-vehicle-class VTABLE at DGROUP:0x42cc+ (`re_out/fist_dat_image.bin` @ img `0x202cc..0x203ce`, seg-0 offsets spaced ~0x30-0x40 = one method-block per vehicle class): tank M1A2 viewport slot = **DGROUP:0x4324 = `0x73b9` -> `FUN_1000_6f1f`**; siblings at 0x42cc/0x4356/0x438e/0x43ce/0x2074e. These are dispatched by the display-element paint-walkers (`FUN_0000_2006` etc.) via `fist_icall_near(0, word[element.word0 + 0x3e18])` (`fist.c:8693`) — i.e. element `word0` = the class-vtable BASE (a DGROUP near-offset), method-slot `+0x3e18`. For the tank slot 0x4324 that implies class base `0x4324-0x3e18 = 0x50c`. Some class methods DO dispatch on the ACCEPT run (`0x2104`@0x4320, `0x71f8`@0x4310, `0x5844`@0x42c0 all appear in the near-trace), but the **viewport-render slot 0x4324 never does** — so the player-vehicle display element with `word0 == 0x50c` (the tank viewport class) is either absent from the render roster or carries a base-lost `word0`. This is the recurring display-element base-loss class; pinning it (dump the roster elements' `word0` during the mission, find the missing/wrong-based viewport element) is the concrete next step.

**FINDING 3 — the op-0x0c "climb" milestone (patches 294-298) is a SPIN ARTIFACT, not frame advancement (asm-verified + experimentally confirmed).** `22dd`'s asm (`objdump` @ 0x22dd) reads d548 as a SIGNED BYTE: `cmp BYTE ds:0x1548,1; jl 0x2307` (enter render loop iff `(int8)byte[0x1548] >= 1`) and the reset test `cmp BYTE ds:0x1548,0x80; je`. **Ghidra typed `DAT_1000_d548` as a WORD** (`undefined2`, `fist.c:9263` `'\0' < DAT_1000_d548`, `:9274` `!= -0x80`), so the C reads the WORD `0x1e81` (d549=0x1e in the high byte) as positive and WRONGLY RUNS the render loop at d548=0x81 (the asm SKIPS it — `0x81`=`-127`<`1`). That wrong loop-run is what re-posts op 0x0c millions of times. **Throwaway proof:** patching `22dd` to faithful signed-byte semantics (`(signed char)`) FREEZES op-posting at `total=539` / `op0c=2` — i.e. with correct `22dd` NOTHING advances the frame, confirming commit 42809f7's "HOLLOW SPIN, frames do NOT advance" and that the `op0c climbs 18654->114362` figure was counting spin iterations, not frames. The byte-fix additionally exposes a DOWNSTREAM runaway MGA fill (`FUN_1000_a5c4 -> [c5c4] -> m_mga 063c/0647/0660` with width `0xffff`, in the `c31e->c336->b39c->a93e` object-render chain) — another entangled base-loss. So the byte-fix is FAITHFUL but cannot land in isolation (it converts alive-spinning into alive-frozen-then-runaway); it must land together with real per-frame advancement + that a5c4 fix.

**FINDING 4 — deliverables 1 & 2 share one root: the ABSENT extender flight-model.** `d99b`'s create-task stores `TCB+8 = &DAT_1000_d548` (`fist.c:32911`), so the EXTENDER writes the d548 render-handshake byte (0x81 = "frame ready") — and the SAME extender PM code computes the per-frame mission camera (TCB `+0x2c/0x30/0x34/0x38/0x3e`, per `docs/oracle_tile3918_producer.md`). Both are extender-32bit-PM (not in FIST.DAT). The port's extender-gate shim already flips d548->0x81, but the real per-frame CAMERA update + the correct handshake TIMING (which frame the viewport method should fire) live in the un-ported flight model. **CAMERA blocker (oracle, unchanged & confirmed relevant):** the mission TCB in the port is `@0x90000` (the op-0x18 MAP-LOAD log); the oracle's live mission camera fields (`docs/oracle_tile3918_producer.md`) vary per frame in the ORIGINAL, driven by that flight model — the port's stay degenerate. A live-camera reconstruction of the extender flight model is the deeper blocker behind deliverable 2; op-0x24-posting (deliverable 1, viewport-element base-loss) is the nearer one and should be pinned first (Finding 2).

**HONEST STATUS:** op 0x24 is NOT yet posted and `9200` does NOT render on the real loop (no terrain-region nonzero/AE to report). This iteration DISPROVED the "op0c climb = frame advancement" reading, PINNED the op-0x24 dispatch blocker to a specific display-element vtable base-loss (`word0`==0x50c / slot `+0x3e18` -> `0x4324` -> `6f1f`), and asm-verified the `22dd` d548 word/byte mistype (a real bug, but entangled — do not land standalone). NEXT: dump the mission render roster to find the base-lost tank-viewport element `word0`, land it with the `22dd` byte-fix + the `a5c4` fill fix as a coordinated set so a frame actually advances and `6f1f->df0e->op24->9200` fires. Reproduce: `FIST_DATADIR=<scratch> FIST_TICK_HZ=25000 FIST_OPHIST=1 FIST_MOUSE="200:160:100:0; 800:160:100:1; 1400:160:100:0; 3000:205:128:0; 3600:205:128:1; 4200:205:128:0; 5400:40:186:0; 6000:40:186:1; 6600:40:186:0; 7200:40:186:0" /tmp/fist_native`.

## DONE (STAGE 3 — **THE 9e4f BACKGROUND-TASK-REGISTRATION CHAIN IS RECONSTRUCTED (patch 298): the mission SIM LOOP now runs CRASH-FREE through the object-timeout scanner — the prior SIGSEGV in `FUN_0000_9db1` (di=0) is GONE, op 0x0c climbs 18654 -> 114362+ crash-free (rc=124 alive, no SIGSEGV) on the genuine BATTLES(160,100)->OK(205,128)->ACCEPT(40,186) AZER1 run, over a 2.5-minute window.** Pristine unchanged (`61453e42`/`0051cb56`/`75c6d726`); `make check` = 298 patches apply; native + wasm green.)

**PATCH 298 (asm-verified vs `re_out/fist_dat_image.bin`; DS=SS=DGROUP=0x1c00).** The chain `461b`(rotator, patch 259) -> `FUN_1000_9e4f` (object-timeout scanner) -> `9d7a` -> `9db1` SIGSEGV'd in 9db1 (`*param_3`, param_3=near-offset-as-host-ptr=0).  Three interlocking base-losses:
- **`FUN_1000_9e4f`** (asm 0x19e4f): two 16-entry timeout scanners (`mov bx,0x9464/0x9524; mov cx,0x10; L: cmpw [bx],0; je next; mov ax,[0x452]; sub [bx+2],ax; cmp ax,0xb4; jb next; mov ax,[0x944c/0x944e]; lcall 9d7a; movw [0x945c],0; decw [0x9450]; movw [bx],0; jmp out; next: add bx,0xc; loop L`).  Ghidra typed the entry table `int*` -> STRIDE 24 (`piVar2+6`) + timestamp at `piVar2[1]`=+4, so after ~10 iterations it walked PAST the 16*12B table into the second table/garbage, dispatched a garbage entry (`word=0`) -> di=0 -> 9db1 crash.  It is a WORD table, **STRIDE 0xc**, object at word[bx], timestamp at word[bx+2]; timed-out iff `word[bx]!=0 && (uint16)(c452-word[bx+2]) >= 0xb4`.  Fixed the pointer type/stride + threaded the dropped per-table type key (word[0x944c]/word[0x944e]) into 9d7a.  Control flow (break/return, unconditional 545c/545e inc, conditional 5454/5456 dec) was already correct.  **PROVEN faithful:** with the correct stride the scanner walks the real 16 entries (probe: scan1 entered, 545c=120>0x77, 5450=3, all entries currently 0 -> no dispatch); the prior "dispatch" was purely the buggy-stride garbage.
- **`FUN_0000_9d7a`** (asm 0x9d7a): dropped the ax type key (fed b1df the 0xf69 lcall segment), hardcoded `in_CF=0` (folded-CF class -> always called 9db1), and passed 9db1 a wrong arg set.  Threaded param_2=type key into b1df, replaced the dropped CF with `g_fist_cf` (set by the patch-258 b1df), and pass 9db1 the entry near-offset (bx survives the 9d7b/9d99 push/pop into 9db1's ebx) + the object near-offset for both di and si.
- **`FUN_0000_9db1`** (asm 0x9db1-0x9e2a): the object initializer.  di==si==object; bx=entry offset=ebx.  Ghidra base-lost di/si (deref'd near-offsets as host int*) and the tail (unaff_CS / in_register_0000000e).  Rebased di/si onto `g_mem+0x1c000`; the tail `lea di,[di+4]; call e1d1` posts the **op-0x54 DISPLAY-LIST command** (e1d1 writes `gs:[TCB+0x3f2]=ebx`=entry-off, aa10=0x54, calls e339 -> `fist_extender_gate` default case = faithful TCB inbox post, return 0) and stores its return byte at object+0xd.

**OBSERVED reached** (461b rotator -> 9e4f scan loop, probe-confirmed).  9d7a/9db1 do not FIRE in the tested window (timeout table empty), but 9db1 was PROVEN reachable by the prior iteration's SIGSEGV there; the reconstruction is asm-verified and correct for when a registered task times out.  **MISSION-PATH ONLY** (d548-gated); the 19 verify flows never enter 459a -> behaviour-neutral.  **NEXT FRONTIER:** no new engine-side crash in the background-task path; the mission loop runs crash-free indefinitely.  The remaining gate is the terrain/camera CONTENT (extender flight model) — the pinned architectural gate (`2ce4`=`0xc30e`=bare `ret`; camera lives in the extender TCB, not the engine sim step).

## DONE (STAGE 3 — **THE 459a COOPERATIVE TIMER-PUMP LINCHPIN IS LANDED (patch 295): `[DGROUP:0x452]` now ADVANCES and the per-tick SIM STEP `(*2ce4)()` EXECUTES on the genuine ACCEPT path (proven: sim-step counter + c452 climb 2->2050+ in lockstep). Two exposed cockpit-HUD/sim base-loss crashes reconstructed (296 = 65c2's c6c8=294d dropped sprite-blit args; 297 = 4691's periodic-housekeeping DGROUP base-losses). The mission crash frontier moved from the FIRST tail block (294d/298a, op0c stalled at 1) THROUGH 4691 to the 9e4f BACKGROUND-TASK-REGISTRATION chain (op0c now climbs 1->18654+). Pristine unchanged (`61453e42`/`0051cb56`/`75c6d726`); `make check` = 297 patches apply; native + wasm green; `bash tools/verify.sh both` = 19/19, native↔wasm bit-identical (all 19 flows are menu/pre-mission -> behaviour-neutral, none enter 459a).**)

**PATCH 295 (the linchpin, asm-verified vs `re_out/fist_dat_image.bin` 0x459a..0x45f6).** 459a is a fixed-timestep loop `2ce2 += (c452-2ce0); for(;2ce2!=0;2ce2--){ SIM STEP (*2ce4)() }`. `c452`=`[0x452]` is bumped only by the INT-8 ISR, driven cooperatively by `fist_timer_pump()` (native drains SIGALRM ticks, wasm one/pump). 459a's outer loop never pumped -> `2ce2 += 0` -> the SIM STEP ran 0x. Added one `fist_timer_pump()` per OUTER do-loop iteration (NOT the inner event drain, which is gated by `for(;2ce2!=0;)` and skipped while c452 is frozen -> would deadlock) = one INT-8 tick per rendered frame, the faithful substitute for the original's async ISR during the render/present. Same cooperative-pump idiom as patches 073/074/137/242/291.

**PROVEN (throwaway 9db1-stub + camera probe, reverted):** with the pump, the sim-step counter climbs 1..2049 in lockstep with `[0x452]` (2..2050) -> **c452 advances + the SIM STEP executes per tick.** BUT the camera fields stay frozen (`7b1c`=0). **KEY FINDING (settles the "frozen camera" question):** the engine sim step **`2ce4`=`0xc30e` = `FUN_0000_c30e` = a bare `ret`** (oracle-confirmed in docs/stage1.md's cockpit-activate note: the reference cockpit frame runs with `2ce4=0xc30e`). So the mission camera is NOT computed by the engine `(*2ce4)()` -- it lives in the **EXTENDER TCB** (voxel-render-is-engine-side memory: `9200` is posted per frame with the camera from the extender TCB, not engine DGROUP). The camera advancing is therefore gated on the extender-side flight model + the object roster, NOT on the engine sim step alone.

**PATCH 296 (asm 0x165c2..0x165e9).** `FUN_1000_65c2`'s `lcall [0x6c8]` (=`FUN_0000_294d` mga transparency sprite-blit) sets `ax=0x1358` (sprite-dir offset) `bx=0x7c3e` (position descriptor); Ghidra dropped both -> 294d resolved a garbage sprite id (0x9b3d) -> 298a wild deref (SIGSEGV, `param_3=0x91d7001`). Threaded `(0x1358,0,0,0x7c3e,0)` (294d param_1<-ax, param_4<-bx; same mapping as patch 190/136). op0c 1->9302.

**PATCH 297 (asm 0x4691..0x46dd).** `FUN_0000_4691` (per-frame ~480-tick housekeeping, gate `[0x452]-[0x6d1a]>=0x1e0`, never true while c452 frozen) had 3 DGROUP near-offset base-losses: `02c5(uVar1>>3, 0x6d0a)` (02c5's `movb [si],0` writes DGROUP:si -> pass g_mem+0x1c000+0x6d0a per its contract, every other caller passes a host ptr); `[(uVar1&7)*2 + 0x6d1c]` host read; and the dropped `(*c298)(si=0x6d0c,bx=0xa,cx=5)` args (order (di,bx,cx,si) per fist.c:28553). op0c 9302->18654.

**NEXT BLOCKER (pinned, full asm) -- the 9e4f BACKGROUND-TASK-REGISTRATION chain `461b->9e4f->9d7a->9db1`.** Dispatched by the 461b per-sim-step rotator (patch 259) once a mission slot registers `FUN_1000_9e4f` (the object-timeout scanner). gdb core: SIGSEGV in `FUN_0000_9db1` fist.c:25652 (`*param_3`, param_3=NULL). Three interlocking base-losses (all asm-verified vs image):
- **`FUN_1000_9e4f`** (0x19e4f): two timeout-scan loops @ DGROUP:0x9464 / 0x9524. Ghidra typed the entry table `int*` (32-bit, stride 24) with a spurious per-entry `piVar2[1]` timestamp; the ASM is a WORD table, **stride 0xc (12B)**, condition `[0x452]>=0xb4` (no per-entry timestamp), and the control flow is mis-structured (`545c++` is UNCONDITIONAL + `5454--` only when `5454!=0`; the port's `break`/`return` after 9d7a are spurious -- the asm continues the 16-entry `loop`). So the port walks PAST the table -> passes a garbage entry (word=0) -> di=0.
- **`FUN_0000_9d7a`** (0x9d7a): `di = word[bx]` (bx=entry ptr) = the object near-offset; calls `b1df(ax=type-key=word[0x944c/0x944e], di)` which returns x86 CF via `g_fist_cf` (already set by the patch-258 b1df: CF=1 = roster full). Ghidra hardcoded `in_CF=0` (the folded-CF class) -> ALWAYS calls 9db1; and dropped the ax type-key -> b1df's `param_1=0xf69` is wrong. Fix: thread g_fist_cf + ax, and pass 9db1 the rebased object `g_mem+0x1c000+di`.
- **`FUN_0000_9db1`** (0x9db1..0x9e2a): the object initializer. Operates on **di==si==the object** (both = the near-offset, preserved through a4a2/b1df); Ghidra base-lost di (param_3, host int*), si (param_2=0xf69, host ptr `[si+0x97/+4/+8]`), and the final `[di+0xd]=al` (unaff_CS). NB it ends with `e1d1(di+4)` -> `e339` = the **EXTENDER GATE op 0x54** (TCB command post `gs:[si+0x3f2]=ebx`), so a faithful 9db1 pulls in the extender-TCB command interface (ebx = the entry offset). This is a bounded MULTI-function reconstruction (9e4f control-flow + 9d7a CF/args + 9db1 object-init + the e1d1/e339 op-0x54 post), NOT a single safe turn -> PINNED, not rushed. Reproduce: BATTLES(160,100)->OK(205,128)->ACCEPT(40,186), `FIST_OPHIST=1` (op0c climbs to ~18654 then SIGSEGV in 9db1).

## DONE (STAGE 3 — **RECON/CHARACTERIZATION: the "op 0x0c climbs = per-frame loop ITERATES" claim (patch 294) is CORRECTED — it is a HOLLOW TIGHT RE-POST SPIN, NOT frame advancement. The frame timer `[0x452]` stays 1 across MILLIONS of op-0x0c posts, the camera/cursor stay frozen, and the per-tick SIMULATION NEVER RUNS. ROOT CAUSE + LINCHPIN PROVEN (the 459a cooperative timer-pump); the exact render-op model + the pinned blocker chain to a real terrain frame are settled below. NO engine/ext/patch change (landing the linchpin alone regresses the mission to a crash cascade -- see below); pristine unchanged (`61453e42`/`0051cb56`/`75c6d726`); `verify.sh` native 18/19 [selplayer-ok = the known load-flake, AE=0 on 3/3 isolated re-runs] + wasm 19/19, native↔wasm bit-identical. New shim diagnostic `FIST_OP0C_BT` (default OFF).**)

**Q1 (loop advancement) -- DECISIVE ANSWER: a TIGHT RE-POST SPIN, frames do NOT advance.** On the genuine BATTLES(160,100)->OK(205,128)->ACCEPT(40,186) AZER1 run (`FIST_OP0C_BT=1`), op 0x0c is posted ~2.7 MILLION times in 30 s with **`[DGROUP:0x452]` (the frame timer) frozen at 1**, the render cursor `[0xea86]` frozen at 0x43, and the camera (`7b1c` heading, TCB+0x2c X / +0x34 alt) frozen -- forever. So patch 294's "op 0x0c climbs 1->489395+ = the loop ITERATES" was measuring the SPIN COUNT; nothing renders or simulates. Backtrace of the poster: `e339<-db99<-41d0<-22dd<-459a<-e714`.

**ROOT CAUSE (asm-verified, `build/fist.c` 459a @0x459a).** 459a's outer loop is a fixed-timestep game loop: `2ce2 += (c452 - 2ce0); for(; 2ce2!=0; 2ce2--) { <SIM STEP: (*2ce4)(); c0ca(update walk); 461b> }`. The per-tick SIM STEP (the flight model + object update that computes the camera heading/altitude) runs ONCE PER ELAPSED FRAME-TIMER TICK. **`c452` (=`DAT_1000_c452`=`[DGROUP:0x452]`) is bumped only when `fist_timer_pump()` drains the cooperative INT-8 tick** (native: drains SIGALRM-accumulated ticks; wasm: one tick/pump; canonical idiom `while(c452==u)fist_timer_pump();` @ fist.c:4430). **459a's inner spin-wait NEVER pumps** -> `c452` frozen -> `2ce2 += 0` -> the `for(;2ce2!=0;...)` sim loop runs ZERO times -> the flight model never runs -> the camera stays degenerate (heading 0, alt 0) -> every frame is identical/static, while 22dd/206f still re-post op 0x0c each iteration. (This is the exact `fist_timer_pump`-in-spin-wait class fixed for the menu cc33 (patch 137) and the not-yet-reached in-mission `e4bb` (patch 291).)

**LINCHPIN PROVEN (throwaway test, reverted).** Adding `fist_timer_pump()` at the top of 459a's outer do-loop makes `c452` advance, `2ce2>0`, and **the SIM STEP RUNS** -- which IMMEDIATELY exposes the next blocker: a SIGSEGV in the cockpit-HUD render `459a->206f->209e->4937->65c2->m_mga_294d->m_mga_298a` (fist_mga.c:6745, wild `param_3=0x91d7001`). `294d` (patch 136 transparency sprite-blit) calls `260c` to resolve a sprite by directory offset; with `c452` advancing, 65c2 renders a tick-phase-gated HUD sprite whose id (`0x9b3d`) resolves to a wild `m_260c_recoff` -> deref off g_mem. So the timer-pump is the CORRECT faithful linchpin, but it opens the DEEP cockpit-HUD/sim render base-loss tail (the documented "residual flaky tail", patches 271-285 class) -- a multi-patch frontier. **Landing the pump ALONE trades the hollow spin (rc=124) for a crash -> NOT landed this iteration; the pump + the render/sim tail is the next deliverable.**

**RENDER-OP MODEL (settled; resolves "op 0x09 vs op 0x0c").** The gate op (`[DGROUP:0xea10]`) is a BYTE offset into the extender dword op-table @`fist_image.bin:0xcb3` (so `gate_op/4` = dword index): gate op 0x0c -> `0x10eb`->`FUN 78f0`; gate op 0x18 -> `0x10ca`->`89b0` map-load; **gate op 0x24 -> `0x82c0`->`FUN 9200`**; gate op 0x40 -> `0xad1e`; gate op 0x44 -> sky. The two terrain renderers (disassembled):
- **op 0x0c -> 78f0 -> 85d0(camera) + 93c0.** `93c0` is a FLAT AFFINE map-warp (two loops, `mov al,[edx+esi]` sampling `ds:0x85b8`+`shld`-warped u/v, `stos WORD`; NO per-column horizon skip) = the OVERHEAD MAP-VIEW (the small grayscale radar minimap bottom-left of the AZER1 frame). Posted by `FUN_0000_db99` (patch 193) every 459a iteration.
- **op 0x24 -> 82c0 -> 9200.** `9200` is the per-column PERSPECTIVE voxel windshield (horizon/sky-skip table + affine texel walk sampling the 64KB tile `DAT_3918`) = the BIG desert terrain filling the AZER1 windshield (the oracle-proven dominant fb writer). Posted by **`FUN_0000_df0e`** (fist.c:33199, `if((param_3[0xd]&0x10)==0) aa10=0x24; e339`) via the per-vehicle viewport render methods `FUN_1000_591b/616e/6a75/73eb/795c`.

**THE REFERENCE AZER1 FRAME = the WINDSHIELD (9200/op 0x24).** The engine's 22dd render-pass cursor walk (`do{ (*near[0x1c000+ [0x4a86]])() }while(c450!=0)`, cursor seeded `word[0x4a88+d549]`) reaches 41d0 (the op-0x0c MAP poster) but **NEVER reaches the cockpit-windshield viewport handler that calls df0e -> so op 0x24 is NEVER posted** (op-histogram `op24(9200)=0` over 2.7M posts). The port therefore posts only the overhead-map op 0x0c, never the windshield op 0x24.

**STATE (op-0x18 map-load, all populated).** colormap `85b8` = 86 distinct/4MB (loaded, no longer `{0,4}`); tile `3918` = nz 65536 / **175 distinct** (built by bc9c/bc06 at op 0x18; oracle 212 -- the residual `85b8`->tile collapse); horizon table `9114`= skip `6 4 3 2 1` (matches the oracle first-column skip=6). **The windshield pipeline WORKS given a valid camera:** `FIST_R3D2_V18` (oracle camera X=609696/Y=1112229/alt=29184/head=19745 + oracle ray tables `3a24/3e24`) drives `8deb->85d0->6980->8120->9200` and renders **coherent desert terrain** (viewport rows5-86 nonzero=25620 distinct=215; top horizon-masked band matches the ref). Caveat: `6980`'s tile rebuild is only 21% vs the oracle tile -- the op-0x18 `bc9c/bc06` builder (175 distinct) is the right one, 6980 is the wrong LOD.

**PINNED BLOCKER CHAIN to a real AZER1 windshield frame (ordered).** (1) **459a cooperative timer-pump** (the linchpin above) -> activates the sim -> then the cockpit-HUD/sim render base-loss tail (`298a/294d/260c` sprite-id, then deeper 271-285-class object methods). (2) Once the sim runs, the flight model computes the live mission camera (heading/alt), fixing the degenerate `7b1c=0`. NOT an upstream-populator bug -> the fix is a FAITHFUL 23d8 that handles the empty descriptor exactly as the original (simulated both seg-0 decodes: original terminates in 193 steps / port's g_mem low-code seg-0 in 99 steps; both crash-free, di/si/bx stay in g_mem bounds via 16-bit wrap).

**WHAT LANDED (patch 294, asm-verified vs `re_out/fist_mga_image.bin` 0x23d8-0x2440).** `FUN_0000_23d8` is the MGAVIDEO cockpit-panel RLE sprite blitter: `es=0xA000; di=0; bp=word[desc] (ctrl seg); dx=word[0x156c] (pixel seg); bx=word[desc+0x1a] (pixel off); ds=bp`; RLE decode with `rep movsw`/`rep movsb` copying `pixel_seg:SI -> ES:DI`, control words `lods`'d from `ctrl_seg:SI`. The decode-COUNT logic (`words=(a>>2)*2+((a>>1)&1)`, `bytes=a&1`, `a=ctrlword>>1`) was CORRECT; only the three segment bases + the control-cursor width were wrong. **Fix:** (1) `fist_mga.c` 23d8 = exact asm translation with 16-bit registers (uint16 wrap) + g_mem-relative segment bases (`ctrl=g_mem+(word[desc]<<4)`, `pix=g_mem+((uint16)_DAT_1000_d56c<<4)`, `fb=g_mem+0xA0000`; descriptor is a DGROUP near-offset value -> `desc=g_mem+0x1c000+(uint16)param_2`); 2e3f/2e8b (clip setup/teardown, pure-DGROUP, safe on the zero descriptor) unchanged. - **9200 horizon/sky-skip table**: `DAT_0000_9114` holds a FLAT image offset (8deb sets it to `(&PTR_748c)[detail]` = 0x7568 for detail 1, the byte skip table baked in the image).  Verified live: `skip[0..4] = 6,4,3,2,1` -- **skip[0]=6 MATCHES the oracle first-column ground truth exactly.**  The per-column read must be `g_mem+fist_ext_base + DAT_9114 + col`, not a host deref of 0x7568.

## DONE (STAGE 3 — **THE "op-0x60 STALL" IS ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED: the engine now ADVANCES OUT OF THE INFINITE SPIN INTO ITS PER-FRAME GAMEPLAY LOOP, and the per-frame TERRAIN RENDER is CONFIRMED ENGINE-SIDE (FIST.DAT), not extender-side.** 2 new patches (242/243), mission-cascade only. `re_out/fist.c` pristine `61453e42`, `fist_mga.c` `75c6d726`, `fist_ext.c` `0051cb56`; default boot rc=0; mainmenu `3a6ff1c5`; verify 19/19. **GATE 1 — the "op-0x60 stall" root cause (asm-verified + gdb-observed, NOT the prior premise).** The handoff premise ("the engine loops posting extender op 0x60 indefinitely") was imprecise: op 0x60 is posted only 16× (the `FUN_0000_9d26` roster re-post of the registered display objects), then execution SILENTLY SPINS with no further ops/traps. gdb-attach to the spin (`FIST_COOP_TICK=1`) pinned it: `app_entry→00d0→cae6→e714→FUN_0000_459a→FUN_1000_35a7(scheduler poll)→FUN_1000_3920(coop yield)` — the spin is in **`FUN_0000_459a`, the IN-MISSION SETUP/VIEW MODAL FRAME LOOP** the ACCEPT cascade runs (both call sites, e714:35299 and :35459, are the mission path — right after `FUN_0000_5d12`, before the `FUN_0000_1631` LOD loader + `FUN_0000_e4bb` HUD loop; NO menu flow reaches 459a). Its per-frame structure: paint (206f) → drain the event queue via the scheduler poll `lcall [DGROUP:0x40a]` (= `FUN_1000_35a7`) → when the queue empties, break to the render (`FUN_0000_22dd`) + next frame. The asm inner drain loop (`0x45d6 lcall *0x40a; 0x45da jb 0x45e1; 0x45dc call 0x1e4b; 0x45df jmp 0x45c8`) breaks on **CF=1 = empty queue**; Ghidra folded the CF guard (`bVar4`) to a constant `false`, so the loop NEVER broke and spun forever polling an empty queue — the exact ec7e/cc33/f338/38bc/38cc CF-fold class. **Patch 242** threads the poll CF via the shim global `g_fist_cf` (set by `FUN_1000_35a7`, patch 073) → 459a now breaks to the render each frame and advances the cascade.

**Advancing the cascade exposed patch 243:** with 459a rendering frames, the per-frame update path `459a→FUN_0000_c0ca→FUN_0000_c0e5` crashed. **`FUN_0000_c0e5`** is the **per-frame display-object UPDATE walk** — it walks the 182-entry runtime table at **DGROUP:0xdfbc** (the SAME table `FUN_0000_d755` walks, patch 169), STRIDE 4, dispatching each object's per-type UPDATE method `word[DGROUP:(word[di]*2 - 0x1bac)]` (near call, DI=object offset live). Ghidra base-lost the walk (`*(int*)*piVar2` = wild host deref, stride 8). Patch 243 reconstructs it faithfully (asm 0xc105-0xc122; DGROUP near offsets; DI threaded as the dispatch arg, mirroring d755). Execution now runs INTO the per-object update methods (first = `FUN_0000_7c1d`).

**GATE 2 — the per-frame TERRAIN RENDER is ENGINE-SIDE (confirmed; the prior "extender op-0x0c" conclusion is superseded for the terrain view).** Static trace of 459a's render tail: **`FUN_0000_22dd`** (called at each frame end, 0x45fd) — when the viewport-active flag `DAT_1000_d548 > 0`, it locks the frame (`lcall [DGROUP:0x554]`), sets `c450=3` (pass count), and loops calling the near render-pass vector `word[*DAT_2000_0a86]` with the viewport rect `DAT_1000_d56a`, then unlocks. `DAT_2000_0a86` is selected from the render-pass fn-offset TABLE at **DGROUP:0x4a88 indexed by `DAT_1000_d549`** (`0a86 = word[&0a88 + d549]`); the passes chain via `0a86` (the `2380/2387/23ba/23be` + `FUN_1000_195d` clip family). The mission VIEW-ACTIVATE **`FUN_1000_67e3`** sets `d548=1, d549=0x1e` (render-pass selector), `d552=0x7aa4` (viewport DST descriptor) — so the terrain render-pass fn = the near-offset word at **DGROUP:0x4a88+0x1e = 0x4aa6**. These are seg-0 (FIST.DAT) near functions dispatched through DGROUP near-vectors — the NovaLogic Voxel-Space raycaster lives HERE, exactly as CLAUDE.md states, NOT in the Doug-Huffman extender. (The extender `93c0/8fa0` op-0x0c path exercised by the retired `FIST_R3D` seam is a flat map-view texture-mapper, a different surface.)

**NEW FRONTIER (blocks reaching 22dd/the render): the per-object UPDATE method cascade.** `c0e5` dispatches each object's think/update method (`FUN_0000_7c1d` = `mov al,[di+0x1d]; mov [di+0xd],al; …` + two CS-relative jump tables 0x7c91/0x7d92 + sub-calls 7cd5/7d0f/7d08/a358 + ext `f69:7f84/aa14`) via the -0x1bac table. Each method reads its object at DGROUP:di and must be reconstructed to (a) take di as arg0 and (b) rebase `[di+off]` → `g_mem+0x1c000+di+off` — the SAME per-method DI-threading + base-loss cascade as the d755 method set (patches 219-232), but for the UPDATE method set. ## DONE (STAGE 3 — **FIRST GENUINE IN-MISSION TERRAIN PIXELS: the op-0x0c voxel render now draws MULTI-COLOUR TERRAIN (18 distinct framebuffer colours, was 1) — the COLORMAP DATA PATH is fully root-caused and repaired end-to-end.** The three upstream defects that collapsed the C32.KLC colormap to a single palette index are found and fixed, asm-verified, via stage-by-stage buffer dumps at the op-0x18 map-load. 3 patches (236/237/238). `re_out/fist.c` pristine `61453e42`; `re_out/fist_mga.c` `75c6d726`; `re_out/fist_ext.c` `0051cb56`; `bash tools/verify.sh` = **19/19** native (native↔wasm bit-identical unaffected); git clean; default boot rc=0; mainmenu `3a6ff1c5`. ACCEPT wiring STAYS HELD — the render is not yet a STABLE frame (a pre-existing heap/tick non-determinism gates whether the mission palette-load runs); no flow/AE claim.)

**THE COLORMAP DATA PATH — FULLY DIAGNOSED (stage-by-stage buffer dumps in the op-0x18 gate).** The C32.KLC colormap DECODES correctly (`FUN_0000_643c` fills the 512×512 buffer at `DAT_85b8` with 248 distinct texels — verified). The uniform-colour render was THREE interlocking defects downstream in the palette-reduction that `9ec0` applies:

- **PATCH 236 — `FUN_0000_9e60`/`FUN_0000_ac70` dropped-register LUT store.** `9e60` builds the 256-entry colormap remap LUT: for each palette entry it calls the nearest-colour search `ac70` and stores the result (asm 9ea5 `mov [edi],bl`, bl = ac70's returned index in EBX/BL, asm acdc `mov ebx,ebp`). Ghidra dropped ac70's ebx return AND its index counter — so `9e60`'s store had no source and Ghidra substituted the phantom `param_1` (= the constant 4 that 9ec0 passes) → every LUT entry = 4. Fix: `ac70` tracks + RETURNS the best index; `9e60` stores it.
- **PATCH 237 — `FUN_0000_6250` PAL.RES resource-archive member resolution (three flat32 bugs).** The reduced palette was empty because `6032` never loaded 532.pal — a MEMBER of the XOR-encoded PAL.RES archive (25120 B). `6250` OPENS PAL.RES but failed to find the member: (1) EDX32 (the AH=3F read buffer, f002c) was `SUB42(DAT_623c,0)` = LOW-16 of the bump-alloc HOST pointer → `ext_addr()` mis-routed both the RESOURCE-header and directory reads (asm 62c0/6314 = full `mov edx,[0x623c]`); (2) the directory-read COUNT (ECX32) was dropped to 0 (asm 6303/6304 `inc ecx; shl ecx,4`, popped at 6313); (3) `DAT_623c` is Ghidra-typed `undefined1*` (for the twin `62fd`), but 6250 reads the header/directory as DWORDs (`lods eax` magic, 16-byte entry stride) → `*DAT_623c` read one byte (`'R'`) ≠ `"RESO"` → magic check failed. Fixed all three (localized (uint*) casts for 6250 only). Now the member RESOLVES (`MATCH op=N size=768` correct), 532.pal loads, `4f60[80..255]` gets real RGB.
- **PATCH 238 — `FUN_0000_ac70` self-modifying-code distance target.** `ac70`'s distance tables `a060/a460/a860` are STATIC weighted-squared LODs (`(31k)²`, `(43k)²`, …) baked in the image; the per-colour distance is `a060[pal_R − T]` where T is the imm8 of `sub al,0xf` (asm acb5) — and ac70's PROLOGUE self-modifies that imm8 (`mov ds:0xacb6,al` writes the target R/2 into the instruction's operand byte). Ghidra doesn't model SMC and baked the literal `0xf` → the distance became TARGET-INDEPENDENT → every colour mapped to the single darkest index (~134). Fix: subtract the same bytes ac70 writes (`bRam0000acb6/acc0/acca` = target R/G/B >>1).

**RESULT (FIST_R3D + FIST_R3D_PROBE, the located op-0x0c render seam).** With all three fixes the colormap remap produces 19 distinct terrain indices (was 1), and the op-0x0c voxel texture-map (`85d0` camera + `93c0`) draws an 18-colour terrain framebuffer — the FIRST genuine in-mission pixels. All three patches are cascade-only (reached ONLY via the op-0x18 mission map-load palette reduction; the 19 menu/dialog flows never load a mission map) ⇒ behaviour-neutral: verify 19/19, default boot rc=0, mainmenu `3a6ff1c5`.

**HOW THE RENDER IS DISPATCHED (static, airtight).** The extender PM gate `FUN_00000f30` dispatches an op via `movzx ebx,bx; mov ebx,[ebx+0xcb3]; call ebx` — the op-table lives at `fist_image.bin:0xcb3` and is read as a **dword at byte-offset `op`** (ops are always multiples of 4, so the dword entries overlap-index cleanly). Decoded entries: op 0x04→`0x10c9`(ret/no-op) · op 0x08→`0x10e0`(`call 8df0`+`call 3931`) · **op 0x0c→`0x10eb`(`call 78f0`) = THE 3D RENDER** · op 0x10→`0x10f7`(`call 7940`→`8fa0`) · op 0x14→`0x1089`(ret) · op 0x18→`0x10ca`(`call 89b0`=map-load, already wired) · op 0x44→`0x10da`(`call 7660`=sky) · op 0x64→`0x786a` / op 0x78→`0x11dd`(KDV present, already wired). op 0x18→0x10ca matches the already-known map-load path — the decode is validated.

**THE RENDER FUNCTIONS (extender image, `re_out/fist_ext.c`).**
- **`FUN_0000_78f0`** (op 0x0c): `90ec = word[TCB+4] + word[TCB+2]*16 + [0x807]` (framebuffer ptr), `90f0/90f4/90f8 = word[TCB+0x1e] / >>1 / word[TCB+0x22]` (viewport w/h), then `85d0()` + `93c0()`. TCB is the current task via `[0xc93]`. (`78e7` is an identical twin; `7940`→`8fa0` is the rotated variant.)
- **`FUN_0000_85d0`** (camera setup): reads camera state from `TCB+0x2c/0x30/0x34/0x38/0x3e` → `90cc/90d0/90d4/90d8/90dc/90e0`; step `90c0 = 0xffffffff / word[TCB+0x3e]`, `90c8 = word[TCB+0x3e]`.
- **`FUN_0000_93c0`** (the texture-map): for each of `90f8` rows × `90f0>>1` cols, writes 2 texels/iter (`CONCAT11`) = `colormap[ ((uVar4>>(0x20-8490))<<8490) + (uVar5>>0x16) ]` sampled from `DAT_85b8` (the colormap, detail `DAT_8490`=0xb ⇒ 2048-wide), stepping the fixed-point cursor `uVar5 += 2*90c0>>1` per column, `uVar4 += 90bc` per row → writes `90f0*90f8` bytes into `90ec`. `8fa0` is the rotated sin/cos variant (samples `90fc/9100` from tables at `944b`/`9650`); `9482` (45418-byte stack frame) is the true voxel-column renderer; `9129` a blend variant. All write to `90ec`. This is textbook NovaLogic Voxel Space, hand-asm, in the Doug-Huffman extender image — exactly like the KDV FMV player (Route 1).

**RUNTIME EVIDENCE (harness: the prompt's 9-event FIST_CASCADE route, ≥170 s).** The mission is reached (op 0x18 MAP-LOAD ×2 `D32.KLC/C32.KLC/532.pal/5.SKY`, op 0x54 roster ×39, op 0x60 ×16); **op 0x0c fires** after the roster. The FIST_R3D diagnostic seam drives the located render: `[r3d] op0x0c RENDER … -> fb nonzero=64000/64000 fb-colors=1`. So the pipeline (`85d0`+`93c0`) executes CRASH-FREE and fills the whole 320×200 framebuffer — the recon and wiring mechanics are validated.

**NEW FRONTIER — the render draws ONE color because the colormap DATA is not loaded (this is the true gate to first pixels).** Two upstream gaps, both UPSTREAM of the now-located render:
1. **Colormap texels absent.** `DAT_85b8` (a valid host heap ptr `0x086f4210`) holds only `{0, 4}` across its whole 4 MB (`04 00 00 00` repeated = dwords==4, ~6% nonzero, 2 distinct byte values). The C32.KLC colormap PIXELS never populate it, even though the extender map-load `89b0`/`643c` completes STRUCTURALLY (`detail[0x8490]=0xb dim[0x8494]=2048`). Suspect: the extender flat-FILEMGR read of C32.KLC delivers no texels (the same 32-bit-flat INT-21 gap flagged for the KDV player), or the LOD upsampler (`bdc4`/`bc06`) reads an empty source. **This is the next iteration's target: verify/repair the C32.KLC→85b8 data path.**
2. **Viewport dims 0.** `word[TCB+0x1e]`/`word[TCB+0x22]` = 0 at op-0x0c time (the FIST_R3D seam forces a bounded 320×200 SCAFFOLD). The faithful source of the mission viewport dimensions (the engine's viewport display-object setup) must be found — candidates: the `22dd` viewport phase, an engine poster writing `TCB+0x1e`, or a per-mission-init step. TCB+2:+4 (render target `0x4ad8:0`) and camera `TCB+0x2c/0x30`=`0xe92e/0x6f1d`, `0x3e`=`0x80` ARE populated, so the viewport-dim write is a specific missing/base-lost store, not a whole-subsystem gap.

The FIST_R3D seam (default OFF, so default boot + the 19 flows are byte-untouched) is the executable record of the located render + the two frontiers; it is NOT accepted (uniform output ≠ recognizable pixels). Detail in `tools/native_main.c` at the op-0x0c gate.

**FRONTIER CORRECTION (hard evidence).** Under `FIST_CASCADE=1 FIST_COOP_TICK=1 FIST_KDV=0` + pattern-init the crash was NOT `3068` but `mga:0x1b5c+36` (RIP 0x811db84) — the per-glyph entry on the status-text path `6686 → 55ae → [DGROUP:0x688] → 1b5c`. A breakpoint at `3068` was hit **0 times**: `6686`'s `lcall [DGROUP:0x6b4]` render dispatch RETURNS (empty geometry) BEFORE its `55ae` status-line draw. So `3068`'s scattered-locals reconstruction, while fully characterized + asm-verified (below), is currently **latent/unreachable** and was intentionally NOT committed (rule: no unobserved reconstructions).

**ROOT CAUSE of the `1b5c`/`1b98` crash — the DGROUP:[0x736] text-state POINTER is a 16-bit NEAR OFFSET, not a host `int**`.** A hardware watchpoint on `g_mem+0x1c736` proved the "pointer" oscillated `0x00264c`/`0x01264c`/`0xff264c` every frame: byte[2] of the 4-byte `_DAT_1000_c736` slot is `0x1c738` = the SEPARATE retrace re-entry counter `DAT_1000_c738`, inc/dec'd by the palette ISR `0b1f`. asm `mov di,ds:0x736; cmp WORD [di],0xa000` → c736 holds a WORD near-offset; `*_DAT_1000_c736` must read a WORD + rebase through `g_mem+0x1c000`. This is the same DGROUP:[0x736] base-loss class patch 131 fixed for the ABOUT-dialog twins `1d23`/`1d5f`; `1b5c`/`1b98` are the HUD/viewport twins.

**233** — `FUN_0000_1b5c` (asm 0x1b5c-0x1b8f): the glyph-entry twin of `1d23`. Read c736 as a `uint16_t` offset, rebase; restore the dropped `word[0x794]=0x7c8`/`word[0x796]=0x140` stores + the `2e12` clip-box-reset tail. **234** — `FUN_0000_1b98` (asm 0x1b98-0x1d22): the 1bpp bit-expander glyph plotter (twin of `1d5f`'s byte-font blitter). Ghidra mis-decompiled the blit into an int-scaled CONCAT11 cascade; rebuilt in the DGROUP-offset model (`SW(o)=word[g_mem+0x1c000+(uint16)(s+o)]`): color-word `[0x792]`, glyph table `SW(6)`+font seg `word[0x71a]`, `di=rowtab[SW(0x1c)]+SW(0x1e)`, `ES=SW(0)<<4`, per-row MSB-first bit expand `px=((bit?0xff:0)&(colorLo^colorHi))^colorHi` → `ES[di++]`, cursor advance `SW(0x14)`/`SW(0xa)+SW(0xe)+SW(0x12)`; control chars 7/8/a/d/c/e/f = the same cursor ops as `1d5f`. Both cascade-only (FIST_CASCADE default OFF) ⇒ behaviour-neutral for the 19 flows (menu/dialog text draws via `1d23`/`1d5f`, never `1b5c`).

**PATCH 210 — the `89b0` `.MEG`-probe dropped-CARRY chain (asm-verified vs `re_out/fist_image.bin`; the fully-root-caused Item 2).**  `89b0`'s four `.MEG` RAM-size probes must leave the detail/LOD word `[0x8490]` at the TCB base (`TCB+0x59`=0x0b), but Ghidra dropped the x86 CF at EVERY level of the extender find-first chain `89b0 -> 5c98 -> 5cc2 -> 5d50 -> 6250` (which signals found=clc / not-found=stc purely through CF): `5c98`'s `in_CF` was hardcoded 0 (=> always "found" 0xffffffff), `5d50` was `void` (dropped its own clc/stc AND `6250`'s bVar6), `5cc2`'s bVar3 was hardcoded false, `89b0`'s uVar10 was hardcoded 0 (=> `if(!(bool)uVar10)` always taken => bump 0x0b->0xc => a 4096² = 8 MB map).  Fix: thread the CF through a dedicated shim global `g_ext_find_cf` (`tools/native_main.c`), written at each function's clc/stc (`6250` 63ca/638f clc / 63f6 stc / 641b-fatal; `5d50` 5e34 clc / 5e38 stc + capture `6250`'s CF into bVar6; `5cc2` propagates 5d50's CF, default stc; `5c98` reads it into in_CF; `89b0` reads it per probe).  The immediate synchronous call order makes a single global exact; distinct from `g_fist_cf` (the engine seg-0 paint CF).  **DUAL-PATH VALIDATED:** for a file that EXISTS (KDV's TITLE.KDV) the 4E succeeds (CF clear at 5d50's int) => the success branch is taken without ever calling 6250 => `g_ext_find_cf`=0, unchanged; only the failing `.MEG` probes now correctly flip to CF=1.  Verified live: default boot plays the KDV intro (395 frames, rc=0) AND the mission cascade keeps `detail[0x8490]=0x0b dim[0x8494]=0x800` (2048, was 0xc/4096).

**MEMORY (Item 3) — the extender heap grown for the per-map LOD build.**  `FIST_MEM_SIZE` 0x200000->0x1000000 (16 MB) and `FIST_EXT_HEAP_TOP` 0x1f0000->0xf80000 (~14 MB heap) in `re_out/ghidra_compat.h` (the established hand-edit precedent for the heap; NOT a patch).  The bc06 (heightmap) 2x2 upsampler expands D32.KLC to ~2 MB; the 9ec0 (colormap) upsampler expands C32.KLC to a 2048x2048 (~4 MB) plane; the 345c doubling-realloc holds old+new simultaneously.  With 2 MB g_mem bc06 overflowed; with 8 MB C32 opened but 9ec0 overflowed; with 16 MB **all three planes' opens are reached (`D32.KLC`/`C32.KLC`/`5.SKY` -> ok)**.  WASM `INITIAL_MEMORY`=64 MB (`tools/build.sh`) already covers the 16 MB static array (checked; no build change).  op-0x18 is FIST_CASCADE-diagnostic-only => behaviour-neutral for the 19 flows (verify both = 19/19, default boot rc=0, mainmenu unchanged).

**THE NEW FRONTIER — `FUN_0000_bdc4` (the sky/colormap 2x2 REVERSE-EXPANSION upsampler, asm 0xbdc4).**  After 5.SKY opens, `89b0`'s third upsample pass (the `if(DAT_0000_395c!=0)` sky block @ fist_ext.c:13336-13345, `9ec0` setup then a `bdc4` loop) SIGSEGVs at fist_ext.c:17079 `*puVar15 = *puVar14` (puVar15 walked ~2 MB past the 16 MB g_mem end).  `bdc4` is a TWO-PHASE reverse in-place 2x2 expansion (asm: phase-1 bdc4..be01 expands columns via `[0xbc90]` palette-nearest-colour matrix `mov eax,[0xbc90]; ah=[esi]; al=[esi]; ah=[eax]`; then `shl [0x5578],1`; phase-2 be05.. expands rows, `edi = param_1 + 2*(X*Y)` reverse walk).  Ghidra's rendering of the multi-phase reverse-walk pointer bases (`puVar15 = param_1 + DAT_0000_5578` vs the asm's accumulated `param_1 + 2*plane`; the `uVar5*-2`/`uVar5*-4` back-steps; the `DAT_0000_bc90>>0x10` matrix-pointer high-half reconstruction) is the suspect.  It is a single function but a pointer-arithmetic-heavy reconstruction whose correctness can only be validated by the map rendering (a wrong offset silently corrupts the terrain) — NOT a safe single-turn commit, HELD per the no-unobserved-reconstructions doctrine.  Sequence after bdc4: the LOD build completes -> mission segment allocates -> STRSEG sets -> the real 4308 walk -> 0x7ac0 geometry -> 60d9 copy -> first in-mission pixels (FIST_FBRAW) -> ACCEPT wired only if crash-free + AE=0.

**THE NEXT FRONTIER — `bc06` LOD-UPSAMPLER HEAP OVERFLOW + the detail-CF chain (items 2/3, both now OBSERVABLE).**  After D32 decodes, `89b0:13253` calls the 2x2 upsampler `FUN_0000_bc06` (asm 0xbc06: doubles X `shl [0x5578],1` then Y `shl [0x557c],1` with rcr-interpolation) in a loop `while ([0x5578] < [0x8494])`, reallocating the buffer via `345c` each doubling. ## DONE (STAGE 3 — THE 5fbe BUFFER-SIZE HOST-PTR BASE-LOSS IS FIXED (patch 208): `D32.KLC` now streams in sane **512-byte** chunks (the buffer SIZE = 0x200), the `643c` OOB write is GONE, and the crash is converted into a precisely-root-caused HANG deeper in the KLC decoder.  `re_out/fist.c` pristine `61453e42`; `re_out/fist_ext.c` pristine `0051cb56`; `bash tools/verify.sh both` = **19/19** (native↔wasm), git clean, default boot rc=124-alive (KDV runs the same extender FILEMGR — no SIGSEGV), mainmenu `3a6ff1c5`.  1 patch (208).  ACCEPT wiring STAYS HELD; C32.KLC/5.SKY NOT yet open / no STRSEG / no flow/AE.)

**PATCH 208 — the init half of the 5fbe frontier (asm-verified, op-0x18-only).**  `FUN_0000_5f17` (image 0x5f17) sets the buffered-reader SIZE word `[0x5560]=0x400`; Ghidra base-lost the bare immediate `0x400` into the host pointer `s_KKKK_0000_0005 + 0x3fb` (= `g_mem+fist_ext_base+0x400`).  In the Route-1 host-pointers-in-slots model that poisoned `[0x5560]` into a huge host-pointer value; the `>>1` yielded garbage and every downstream `[0x5560]+[0x5564]` / `[0x5560]-x` in `5fbe`/`643c` addressed off-buffer → the `643c` OOB write `0xc3ee4a8` (the bogus size == the observed 68475568-byte read count).  asm ground truth: `5f17 mov [0x5560],0x400 ; shr ; cmp 0x400/jbe → static 0x5898`.  `[0x5560]` is an INTEGER size (0x200 after the shr); `[0x5564]`=host bufptr (`&DAT_5898`), `[0x5568]`=host end.  Fix stores the literal `0x400` (bound `< 0x401`) so `[0x5560]` stays a small integer masquerading as a pointer → no macro/type ripple.  `ext_addr` (fist_dos.c) resolves the read-buffer DX (a host ptr ≥0x10000) through as-is, so the `3F read n=512` lands in the static buffer correctly.

**THE PRECISELY-MAPPED NEXT FRONTIER — the `643c` KLC-heightmap RLE decoder drops 5fbe's RETURN cursor at every refill site (the second half of the 5fbe frontier).**  `FUN_0000_5fbe` is the buffered reader; **both its exit branches converge at asm `602b mov esi,[0x5564]; ret` → it ALWAYS returns `esi = [0x5564]` (buffer START, after compact+refill).**  Ghidra emitted the return as garbage `CONCAT31(in_register_00000009,param_3)` (fist_ext.c:9026 — the fix is `return (undefined8*)DAT_0000_5564;`).  Every caller in the decoder does `if ([0x5568] <= cursor) cursor = 5fbe(...,cursor,...);` (asm `cmp esi,[0x5568]; jb skip; call 5fbe; skip: <use esi>`) but the decompile **drops the return and keeps the stale cursor**, so after the first refill it reads off-buffer.  VERIFIED live: capturing the return at the HEADER read (fist_ext.c:10179 `piVar15 = (int*)FUN_0000_5fbe(...)`) makes `DAT_0000_5578` (map dim) read correctly (the asm reads magic+dims via LODS from 5fbe's returned esi at `659a`: `lods→[0x5578]`, `lods→[0x557c]`, `mul→[0x937]`) and converts the hang into a `bc06` SEGV — i.e. the LOD upsampler now runs but walks a mis-decoded heightmap.  **The decoder is THREE Ghidra-split functions** `643c`(10136-10436) + `6481`(10437-10593) + `6542`(10594-…) — one RLE decoder whose branches Ghidra promoted to separate funcs; **9 refill sites** (fist_ext.c:10179/10197/10219/10495/10524/10539/10650/10661/10716), the 5th positional arg is the cursor at each (`puVar16`/`puVar17`/`pbVar12`/`pbVar13`/`pbVar10`/`param_5`).  Several sites also carry a MANGLED `return puVar5` where the asm CONTINUES the decode loop (`65ff cmp esi,[0x5568]; jb 6606; call 5fbe; 6606:…` — no return).  Reconstructing this = thread the cursor-return at all 9 sites + fix the `return`→continue control flow; verifiable by the terrain decoding cleanly → **`C32.KLC`+`5.SKY` open** → mission segment → STRSEG.  Bounded but multi-function + control-flow-sensitive (a wrong site silently corrupts the heightmap) — NOT a safe single-turn commit, held per doctrine.

**ITEM 2 (the `.MEG`-probe dropped-CF) — root-caused, gated behind the 643c fix for observability.**  `89b0`'s four `.MEG` RAM probes should leave the detail word `[0x8490]` at the TCB base (`[0xc93+0x59]`=0x0b) but land it at 0xc because the find-first CF is dropped.  The chain `89b0 → 5c98 → 5cc2 → 5d50 → 6250` uses the x86 CF (each function `clc`/`stc`) as the found/not-found signal — Ghidra dropped it at EVERY level (`5c98`'s `in_CF` is hardcoded 0 → always returns 0xffffffff "found"; `5d50` drops `6250`'s `bVar6`; `5cc2` returns no CF).  `5d50` DOES compute the 4E result correctly in `uRam000f0012` (= `R_CF`, set by the shim `set_cf`, at fist_ext.c:8719) — the fix is to propagate that found-status up the chain (a `g_fist_cf`-style thread, but touches the shared FILEMGR that KDV uses, so must keep the default boot rc=0).  Forcing detail to base did NOT clear the hang (proving the hang is the 643c decode, not the LOD size), so item 2 has no independently-verifiable effect until 643c decodes — held.


## DONE (STAGE 3 — THE bc90 COLOUR-DISTANCE-MATRIX GATE IS CLEARED AND **D32.KLC OPENS**: the palette-reduction pass `9f70`→`bc9c`⇄`ac70` now completes on the real 64 KB matrix, and the extender terrain loader `643c` OPENS the map heightmap `D32.KLC` (+ the PAL.RES/532.pal palette archive) — the opentrace milestone's first file.  The fix is a SHIM change (no engine patch): drive the LOCATED extender task-setup allocator `FUN_0000_84c0` + a task-load allocator reset.  `re_out/fist.c` pristine `61453e42`; `bash tools/verify.sh` = **native 19/19 AND wasm 19/19** (both after the change), git clean, default boot crash-free (RC=124-alive, no SIGSEGV), mainmenu `3a6ff1c5` unchanged.  ACCEPT wiring STAYS HELD; C32.KLC/5.SKY NOT yet open / no STRSEG / no flow/AE claim.)

**WHERE THE ORIGINAL ALLOCATES THE MATRIX (the located gate).**  `FUN_0000_bc9c` (palette-reduction, `re_out/fist_ext.c`) reads its 256×256 nearest-colour matrix base from `DAT_0000_bc90` (asm `bc9c mov ecx,[0xbc90]; …; mov [ecx],bl` with CL/CH = col/row, so `[0xbc90]` must be a **64 KB-aligned** pointer — the low 16 bits ARE the index).  `image[0xbc90]=0` in this port → near-NULL write → SIGSEGV.  The original allocates it in **`FUN_0000_84c0`** (`re_out/fist_ext.c`, image 0x84c0): eight `FUN_0000_36bf` bump-allocs (via `FUN_0000_3772`, heap `[0x90b]/[0x90f]`) — the 64 KB matrix→`[0xbc90]` (align 0x10000), 16 KB→`[0xbc94]`, `85c0`/`85c4`/`3909`/`390d`/`3918`, and the size-0 **bc98 FREE-CHECKPOINT** LAST.  `84c0` is called from the extender exec/task-load spawn routines (`FUN_0000_0dee`/… at 0x1381/0x1495, right before the INT-21 AH=4B EXEC) — an init path this port had never driven.

**THE FIX (SHIM, `tools/native_main.c` op-0x18 gate + `re_out/ghidra_compat.h` heap).**  In `fist_extender_gate` op 0x18, once per mission (guarded), BEFORE `89b0`: (1) **task-load reinit** `*[0x2f50]=0` so `84c0`'s first `36bf` calls `FUN_0000_2f7c` (`2f50=[0x90b]`, `2f54=0`) — a FRESH registry; (2) call `m_ext_FUN_0000_84c0(inbox)`.  Why the reset is load-bearing (root-caused with a shim registry probe): the port reaches op 0x18 with a STALE size-0 `bc98` already registered at index 0 (a leftover checkpoint from the intro/menu heap, `2f54=1`, cursor already at base).  Without the reset, `84c0`'s bc90..3918 register at indices 1..7 and `84c0`'s own `bc98` registration DEDUPES away (36bf duplicate-check) → `bc98` stays at index 0 → `89b0`'s opening `FUN_0000_3322(&bc98)` (free-to-checkpoint; `3661` finds bc98 at idx 0) frees the WHOLE registry (indices 0..7), and `3376` (compacting free) ZEROES `[0xbc90]` → bc9c NULL-writes.  With the reset: `3661(&bc98)`→idx 7, `3322` frees only bc98, **bc90 preserved (0x082b0000, 64 KB-aligned host ptr)** — verified slot-for-slot via the registry probe.  Heap `FIST_EXT_HEAP_TOP` grown 0x160000→0x1f0000 (+ `FIST_MEM_SIZE` 0x180000→0x200000) to hold 84c0's ~300 KB of persistent buffers + the per-map D32 heightmap.  `ext_addr()` (fist_dos.c) already treats heap pointers (≥0x10000) as raw host pointers, so the extender heap can be arbitrarily large.

**OBSERVED (FIST_CASCADE 3-click BATTLES→OK→ACCEPT from repo root):** `[ext] op 0x18 MAP-LOAD` → `84c0` runs (bc90=0x082b0000) → the .MEG RAM probes → `PAL.RES`/532.pal palette archive → `9f70`→`bc9c`⇄`ac70` palette reduction COMPLETES (was the SIGSEGV) → `[ext] 3D open 'D32.KLC' -> ok` + read.  Behaviour-neutral for the 19 covered flows (op 0x18 is mission-cascade only; the gate is guarded and never posted on menu/mission-select paths).

**NEXT FRONTIER — the extender FILEMGR BUFFERED STREAMING READ (`FUN_0000_5fbe`), a NEW flat32 host-pointer base-loss.**  After D32.KLC opens+reads, `643c` crashes in its palette/data copy (OOB write to 0xc3ee4a8): the buffered reader's buffer-size/pointer state (`PTR_LAB_0000_5560` = buffer SIZE, `DAT_0000_5564` = buffer PTR, `DAT_0000_5568` = buffer END, set in the mangled init around image 0x8888) is host-pointer-arithmetic-corrupted — the bogus `PTR_LAB_0000_5560` = the observed 68475568-byte read count, and `piVar15 = PTR_LAB_0000_5560 + DAT_0000_5564` (size+ptr) walks OFF the buffer (should be the buffer START).  This is the documented "extender FILEMGR 32-bit-flat buffered read" frontier (the shared 16-bit reg-file `(short)`-truncation class) — KDV never exercised it (direct reads).  Reversing `5fbe`/`5560`/`5564`/`5568` + the map's full LOD build (detail `8494=1<<8490`, 2048² = 4 MB, needs the `.MEG`-probe `uVar10` dropped-CF fix so `8490` stays at the base level + a multi-MB heap) is the path to C32.KLC/5.SKY opens → mission segment → STRSEG → 0x7ac0 geometry → render.  ACCEPT wiring STAYS HELD; no flow/AE claim.

## DONE (STAGE 3 — THE EXTENDER FILEMGR MAP-LOAD IS WIRED AND EXECUTING: op 0x18 → `0x10ca`/`0x89b0` now runs the real decompiled extender map-load setup, the 32-bit-flat FILEMGR resolves the staged map names, and the XOR-encoded **PAL.RES resource archive OPENS** (the palette-member path the KDV never exercised).  4 new asm-verified patches (204–207) advance the map-load through the free-group prologue, the LOD memory probe, the palette resource-archive lookup, and the palette-sort/distance tables.  `re_out/fist.c` pristine `61453e42`; `bash tools/verify.sh both` = **19/19** native↔wasm bit-identical, git clean, default boot rc=0, mainmenu `3a6ff1c5`. **WHAT WAS WIRED (op 0x18, the located gate).**  `fist_extender_gate` (tools/native_main.c) now handles op 0x18 (was: logged + returned 0): it mirrors the op-table entry `fist_image.bin:0xcb3[0x18] → 0x10ca` (`movl $0x10a0,ds:0xd82; call 0x89b0`) by pointing the extender current-TCB `[fist_ext_base+0xc93]` at the ENGINE mission TCB (`DAT_2000_aa2c`, where `FUN_1000_63b8` staged `TCB+0x7a='D32.KLC'`/`+0x8a='C32.KLC'`/`+0x9a='532.pal'`/`+0xaa='5.SKY'`), setting `g_fist_ext_int=1` (route INT 21h through the flat-mode FILEMGR), and calling the real decompiled `m_ext_FUN_0000_89b0`.  Runs once/mission (guarded), cascade-only ⇒ behaviour-neutral for the 19 flows (op 0x18 is never posted on the menu/mission-select paths; verify 19/19 unchanged, default boot rc=0).  Observed live (FIST_CASCADE 3-click route): `[ext] op 0x18 MAP-LOAD: TCB @0x90000 'D32.KLC'/'C32.KLC'/'532.pal'/'5.SKY' [+0x54]=50 [+0x59]=0b` → the LOD probe (`4E find-first '4.MEG'/'8.MEG'/'16.MEG'/'40.MEG'` all FAIL, as expected — those are memory-size probe names) → `3D open 'PAL.RES' -> ok` (the palette resource archive).

**THE FILEMGR ALREADY STANDS (KDV proved it; op 0x18 reuses it).**  The extender 32-bit-flat INT-21 (`fist_dos.c` `dos_int_ext`, gated on `g_fist_ext_int`) handles AH=1A/3D/3F/3E/42/4E/4F addressing `g_mem+EDX` (flat, `[0x807]=0` identity) with `open_ci()` case-insensitive resolution; the path tables (`[0x6234]` root="" / `[0x622c]` resource-dir / `[0x927]` DTA / `[0x6238]` alt) are seeded in `ext_module_init`.  This is the SAME path that loads TITLE.KDV on the DEFAULT boot (`4E find-first 'TITLE.KDV' -> ok`, `3D open -> ok`, 395 frames, EOF, close → menu), so the "KDV OPEN blocker" premise is already resolved — the FMV intro loads through it end-to-end.  The NEW code paths op 0x18 exercises are (a) the resource-archive branch of the resolver (`5cc2→5d50→6250`) and (b) the terrain/palette build in `89b0`, neither of which KDV ever touched.

**FOUR ASM-VERIFIED PATCHES (all real `__allregs`/flat32 base-loss bugs never exercised until op 0x18 was wired):**
- **204** `FUN_0000_3322` (MEMMGR free-group, `89b0`'s prologue): the free-loop start index is `3661`'s found block INDEX (ESI, published to `[0x2f60]`), asm `3339 sub ecx,esi` — Ghidra dropped the ESI and threaded the caller's garbage as `param_2`, wild-freeing → SIGSEGV.  Same class as patch 090 (3376).
- **205** `FUN_0000_5d50` (FILEMGR resource-archive lookup): pass the ORIGINAL member-name pointer to `6250` (the archive opener), which reads it via `lodsb` from ESI (saved 5d51 `push esi` / restored 5df6 `pop esi` across the uppercase-copy loop).  Ghidra dropped the save/restore and threaded `5cc2`'s `CONCAT22(extraout,R_DX)` garbage → wild deref.  **This is the fix that makes `PAL.RES` OPEN** (532.pal is a member of the XOR-0xaceddead-encoded PAL.RES archive, not a standalone file).
- **206** `FUN_0000_9f05`/`9f10` (palette luminance-sort): base the flat32 module offset `0x5598` (palette buffer `&DAT_0000_5598`) — asm `9f18 mov esi,0x5598; add esi,eax*3`; Ghidra rendered it as a bare int → near-NULL pal ptr → wild deref.
- **207** `FUN_0000_ac70` (palette-distance tables): base+rescale the three dword tables at module offsets `0xa060`/`0xa460`/`0xa860` (asm `acb7 mov edi,[0xa060+eax*4]; add [0xa460+eax*4]; add [0xa860+eax*4]`, byte stride 4).  Ghidra base-lost `0xa060` to a bare int (SIGSEGV) and over-scaled the a460/a860 forms as `undefined4* + N*4` (=N·16 bytes, 4×).

**THE NEXT FRONTIER — `FUN_0000_bc9c` (the palette-reduction color-distance MATRIX) needs its 64 KB buffer allocated.**  `89b0`'s palette-reduction pass (`9f70 → bc9c ⇄ ac70`, then `bd0e`/`bd62`) builds a symmetric 256×256 nearest-colour matrix.  bc9c's asm `bc9c mov ecx,[0xbc90]` loads the matrix BASE from `DAT_0000_bc90`, and the inner writes are `bcf2 mov [ecx],bl` (direct) + `bcf4 xchg cl,ch; bcf6 mov [ecx],bl` (transpose), with CL/CH the (col,row) indices in the low 16 bits of ECX.  **`image[0xbc90] = 0` and no code writes it in the reachable path → ECX is a near-NULL pointer → SIGSEGV on the matrix write.**  The matrix is 64 KB (> the 0xbf90 module image), so it must be HEAP-allocated (the extender's own bump allocator `[0x90b]/[0x90f]`) and its pointer stored at `[0xbc90]` — an allocation the extender init does that this port has not yet driven.  This is the gate between the palette-reduction pass and the terrain opens: `89b0` calls `6032`(532.pal, done) → **`9f70` palette-reduce (bc9c matrix = HERE)** → `ac70` → `FUN_0000_643c`(**D32.KLC** heightmap) → `643c`(**C32.KLC** colormap) → sky.  So the KLC/SKY opens are one subsystem (the bc9c matrix allocation + the reduction pass) upstream.  Once the map loads, the mission data segment allocates and the engine's STRSEG (`word[DGROUP:0x70]`, currently 0) sets for the real 4308 walk → 0x7ac0 geometry → 60d9 → render.


## DONE (STAGE 3 — THE 8daa "GATE" IS CLEARED (patches 201/202/203) AND THE STATED FRONTIER IS CORRECTED WITH HARD EVIDENCE: 8daa is NOT the gate — it is a symptom of a garbage table-walk.  The mission-viewport walk `e006→42cc→4308→8b07→8daa` runs `4308` over `STRSEG:0x802`, but STRSEG (`word[DGROUP:0x70]`) is **0** during that walk, so 4308 dispatches ~110 GARBAGE entries (segment-0 string data: `ax=0x4b55`="UK", `cx=0x4a4e`="NJ", …) to random code offsets → the crashes (8daa/a80b/7bcf/…) are all garbage-dispatched. Ghidra base-lost both (`*(int*)(0x8b03)` SIGSEGV).  **202** `FUN_1000_a80b` (called `a80b(0xf,DAT_2000_2d34)`): copies object fields `[di+N]` into the d548-family globals; all `[di+N]` are DS-relative, base-lost → with di=0 the first read faulted at host 0x26.  **203** 8daa's d550 detail-LOD lookup base-lost the object read `[di+0x89]` AND the STRSEG table read `gs:[idx+0x2700]` (gs=`word[0x70]`). **THE TRUE ROOT (deterministic, evidence-based).**  `FIST_GEOMDUMP` prints `[geom] STRSEG word[0x70] 2d74 -> 0000 (at op 0x18)`: the models load fine while STRSEG=0x2d74 (26 opens), then at extender op 0x18 STRSEG becomes 0 and stays 0 through the 4308 viewport walk.  The cascade order (patch-172 harness, build/fist.c ~35160) is `4754`(FSG parse) → **`63b8`**(map staging) → `e006`(→4308→8daa) — so 63b8 runs BEFORE the 8daa cascade.  **63b8 WORKS PERFECTLY** (traced): `byte[DGROUP:0x7a13]=1` (set by the FSG BINF handler d8d3), and it stages the map names into the extender TCB — **`TCB+0x7a='D32.KLC'`, `+0x8a='C32.KLC'`, `+0x9a='532.pal'`** (it rewrites `.PCX`→`.KLC` in the DAT_2000_39cd/39dd name strings, then copies into the TCB asset-name slots).  So the map NAMES are correctly staged; the gate is that the extender op that CONSUMES them never loads the file.

**THE MAP-LOAD GATE — LOCATED (extender op 0x18 → the extender FILEMGR, the SAME documented gap as KDV).**  The extender op-table @ `re_out/fist_image.bin:0xcb3` (4-byte, byte-indexed): op 0x18 → `0x10ca` (`mov ds:0xd82,0x10a0; call 0x89b0`).  **`0x89b0`** = the map-load setup: `ebx=[0xc93]`(current-TCB); `movzx ecx,[ebx+0x59]`; then FOUR calls to the extender FILEMGR **`0x5c98`** (esi=0x84a0/0x84a6/0x84ac/0x84b3 name buffers; `jb`=open-fail), setting the detail/LOD word `ds:0x8490`.  This is the extender's own 32-bit-flat FILEMGR (`FUN_00005cc2/5d50` class) — the ALREADY-DOCUMENTED frontier that also blocks the KDV OPEN (needs the FILEMGR path tables initialized + a 32-bit-flat INT-21 addressing `g_mem+EDX`, `[0x807]=0` identity; the shared reg-file is 16-bit `DS:DX`).  op 0x18 is currently unwired in `fist_extender_gate` (logged + return 0), so `0x89b0` never runs → no `D32.KLC`/`C32.KLC`/`532.pal`/`5.SKY` open → mission segment unallocated → STRSEG=0 → 4308 garbage-walk.  **NEXT FRONTIER = wire op 0x18 → the extender map loader `0x10ca/0x89b0` and stand up the extender FILEMGR (32-bit-flat INT-21 + path-table init) so `0x5c98` opens D32.KLC.**  This is the single gate to the mission segment / STRSEG / real 4308 handler table / geometry / render.  The 8daa-family fixes (201/202/203) are then already in place for the real (non-garbage) walk.  ACCEPT wiring STAYS HELD; no flow/AE claim.

Patches 197/198 were correct but masked by this third bug — d501 has now yielded three stacked latent defects (read-buffer host-ptr, dispatch-table pointer-scaling, flag width), all on one never-before-executed path.

**PATCH 200 — the DCBS unit-database record chain b21d/b1a2/d81e/43c1 (DGROUP near-offset base-losses + the dropped DI slot thread).**  With 199 the first DCBS record dispatched d7e1→d81e→b1a2→b21d and SIGSEGV'd (pattern-init deterministic, core-verified).  asm-verified reconstructions (0x1b21d/0x1b1a2/0xd81e/0x43c1): **b21d** = roster INSERT — friendly slot table DGROUP:0xa022 stride 0x37 + flags 0xe2f7[] vs enemy 0xc05c stride 0xfb + flags 0xe38d[] keyed on team bit byte[obj-0x19ec]&1; word[di]=obj, word[di+2]=idx; returns DI + CF(full@0x96) via g_fist_cf.  **b1a2** = wrapper publishing {di,cx} into the 0x9fbc/0x9fbe index tables word[idx*2] + zeroing the slot body (0x33/0xf7 B).  **d81e** = per-record reader: di=b1a2(...); save word[di+2]; INT-21 read (a9c2-2) bytes into DS:(di+2); restore; 43c1(di).  **43c1** = unit-record installer (flags [di+0x16]/[di+0x17], cell-index table DGROUP:0x6d3c registration, c296). **THE THREE GEOMETRY-PROBE QUESTIONS -- ANSWERED WITH GDB GROUND TRUTH (FIST_COOP_TICK cascade).**
- **(a) Why is `FUN_0000_60d9`'s one-shot branch never reached?  IT IS REACHED -- the premise was a coop-tick/timing artifact of the geomdump.**  gdb breakpoints prove `4854 -> 47e0 -> 60d9` all fire once; `47e0` sets `DAT_2000_3aa2 = 0xffff` after `60d9` runs.  60d9 copies the 0x1c-byte SRC descriptor `word[DGROUP:0x7ac0] -> DST word[0x7aa4]` via mga `[0x690]`=0x22bf -- BUT it copies from an ALL-ZERO source, so DST stays 0.  The pointer `d552=0x1552` IS set to 0x7aa4 by `FUN_1000_67e3` (from the 4937/209e paint walk).  So the copier/pointer machinery works; the DATA is empty.
- **(b) What populates the 0x1c-byte SRC descriptor at `DGROUP:0x7ac0`?  NOTHING -- proven by `awatch` over the whole cascade: the ONLY accesses to 0x7ac0 are READS inside mga 0x22bf (called from 60d9); there is no writer anywhere.**  It is meant to be filled by the mission MAP/viewport setup, which is gated on the map load -- which never happens (see (c)).
- **(c) Why do `d548`/`d552` revert after op 0x18?  Because the map never loads -- THIS IS THE WHOLE STORY, exactly as the task hinted.**  Full open-trace confirms NO `D32.KLC`/`C32.KLC`/`532.pal`/`5.SKY` is EVER opened.  Root chain (all asm-verified vs `re_out/fist_dat_image.bin`): the mission-start `e87a`/patch-172 cascade calls `FUN_0000_4754 -> FUN_0000_d501` to parse the selected battle's `.FSG` (AZER1.FSG); d501 reads a `"SHDR"`-magic header then dispatches a typed-chunk loop; the **BINF** chunk handler `FUN_0000_d8d3` reads the map-name block (`"D32.KLC","C32.KLC","532.pal","5.SKY"`, verified present in AZER1.FSG at file 0x29b8) into `DGROUP:0x79cd` and sets `byte[DGROUP:0x7a13]=1`; `FUN_1000_63b8` (gated on `byte[0x7a13]==1`) then stages those names into the extender TCB for the terrain/palette/sky load, which populates `0x7ac0`.  **NONE of it ran because d501 could not even parse its own header.**

**PATCH 197 -- the d501 FSG chunk-header read buffer (host-ptr-into-DOS-register, patch 077/156/162 class).**  gdb: d501 opens AZER1.FSG -> ok (CF=0, handle=8) but the 6-byte magic read returned `'????'` (0x3f3f) not `"SHDR"`, so the magic check failed and d501 returned immediately.  asm 0xd51e/0xd53e do `mov dx,0xe976` (the DGROUP read buffer offset); Ghidra rendered the immediate as the HOST pointer `&DAT_2000_a976` (g_mem+0x2a976), whose low-16 lands in the shim's `R_DX` = garbage -> the read missed 0xe976.  Fix: write the DOS offset `0xe976` at both reads.  (Patch 162 fixed exactly this class in the chunk HANDLERS d7b5/d7e1/d87f but missed d501's own two reads -- d501 was never on a live path before.)  OBSERVED: magic read now yields `"SHDR"`, magic passes, chunk loop entered.

**PATCH 198 -- the d501 chunk-type DISPATCH-TABLE match (pointer-scaling, int-typed-table class).**  With 197 the loop runs but no chunk MATCHES.  The dispatch table at `DGROUP:0xe9e6` is 7 entries of 6 BYTES `{typeLo:u16, typeHi:u16, near-handler:u16}` (asm 0xd549 walks stride 6, compares 16-bit typeLo/typeHi, handler at [entry+4]).  Ghidra typed the walker `int*` (4-byte): `*piVar5`(4B, 0x52444853 for "SHDR") never equals the 2B `a976`(0x4853); `piVar5[1]/[2]` read the wrong fields; and `piVar5+3` strides 12 B = TWO entries.  Fix: WORD-offset walk, stride 6, 16-bit compares, dispatch `[off+4]` (all 7 handlers provably `clc` -> the asm `jb 0xd565` seek-on-CF is never taken -> a match falls to `LAB_0000_d57e`).  OBSERVED (gdb, 197+198): the SHDR chunk now MATCHES and dispatches its handler `FUN_0000_d7b5` -- the first time the FSG typed-chunk dispatch fires (table@0xe9e6 e0={4853,5244,h=d7b5} vs chunk a976=4853 a978=5244, confirmed at runtime).

**NEW FRONTIER (the map-open is NOT yet observed -- honestly gated on a pre-existing downstream bug).**  After SHDR dispatches, `d7b5` does not return: the cooperative timer pump re-enters the uninitialised-geometry MISSION PAINT during d7b5's chunk read (`e714 -> 459a -> FUN_0000_1e4b -> thunk_FUN_0000_1eb4 -> fist_hit1eb4 -> FUN_0000_4aaa -> MGAVIDEO FUN_0000_25d1`), which SIGSEGVs at `fist_mga.c:6012` `*(int*)(param_1*2 + 0x7c8)` -- a rowtable near-offset `0x7c8` used as a HOST pointer (mga hit-test base-loss, the c684/26de 0x7c8-table class of patches 119/121), and/or spins in the op-0x4c present pump.  So DCBS..BINF are pre-empted and `byte[0x7a13]` / the D32.KLC open is not yet reached.  NEXT (ordered): (1) reconstruct the `FUN_0000_25d1` (mga hit-test) `0x7c8` rowtable base-loss + any op-0x4c present-spin so the mission paint is crash-free with zero geometry; (2) then d501 parses SHDR->DCBS->PATH->STMP->PINF->BINF->TERM, BINF sets 0x7a13, 63b8 stages the map names, and D32.KLC/C32.KLC/532.pal/5.SKY OPEN -> the `0x7ac0` viewport geometry populates -> 60d9 copies REAL data -> c33c/8329/`[ds:0x6b4]` model render + terrain. - **MODELS DO LOAD (milestone confirmed):** `FIST_CASCADE=1` 3-click route (from repo root) opens the full roster `-> ok` (M1_A `.M00/.M08/.MAL`, APACHE/HIND/T80_*/BMP_*/M3_*/MUZZLE/SHOT/EXPLODE/ARTILL/TARGETS `.MAL`, +AZER1.FSG/FSW, LOADING.MS3, MSPRITE*.BIN).  (NB: the "[dos] 3D open" trace is gated on `FIST_TRACE_TRAPS`, NOT `FIST_FFTRACE`; and the log has one binary-garbage `.MAL` name so `grep` needs `-a`.  One residual garbage name-build FAIL remains — a minor c550 index edge, not the 52→0 roster.)  The `STRSEG:0x802` table is STATIC engine-image data (linear 0x2d740+0x802): entry0 `{ffff,0b93,0dde,vec=4397}`, entries1-5 `{0008,0x38/0x40/0x48/0x50/0x58,0/2/4/6/8,vec=183f}` — 4308 walks it and 183f loads the LODs.
- **GEOMETRY DOES NOT POPULATE (the overturn):** across the entire cascade `word[0x7ac0]`(SRC)=0, `word[0x7aa4]`(DST)=0, and the 0x1c-byte SRC descriptor is all-zero.  **`DAT_2000_3aa2` stays 0x0000 → `FUN_0000_60d9` (the SRC→DST copier via mga `[0x690]`=22bf, patch 173) NEVER RUNS** (its caller `47e0`'s one-shot `3aa2==0` branch is unreached), and the mission-viewport flags `d548`(0x1548)/`d552`(0x1552) briefly become 1/0x7aa4 at op 0x18 then REVERT to 0/0 by op 0x4c — the viewport element is set up then torn down.  **Image-wide scan of the immediate `0x7ac0` (`c0 7a`) in the code region = exactly 2 sites (0x47f9 `bb c0 7a` inert c68c-noop; 0x60df `be c0 7a` = 60d9's `mov si,0x7ac0` READ-for-22bf) — NOTHING writes the SRC descriptor.**  So the mission-screen viewport-element creation that should fill the 0x1c-byte `0x7ac0` src (note `0x7ac0 = 0x7aa4+0x1c`, adjacent structs) either never runs or writes via an unlocated pointer path. **STEPS 2/3 — THE OBJECT-RENDER DISPATCH + THE `[ds:0x6b4]` METHOD, MAPPED STATICALLY (asm-verified vs `re_out/fist_dat_image.bin` / `fist_mga_image.bin`).**  Enumerated without reaching the dispatch (more reliable than a runtime read):
- **`0x0c06d`** = far `call cs:[bx+0xc073]`; the cs-table at 0xc073 (near-offset words) = `{0xc084, 0xc0b0, 0xc0b6, 0xc083(ret), …}`.  Handler 0xc084 = `push ax; push dx; lcall [DGROUP:0x538]; lcall [DGROUP:0x518]` (per-object far render methods).
- **`0x0c33c`** = a 10-entry near-vector DISPATCH LOOP over the handler table at `DGROUP:0xe6f5` (cursor `word[0xe6f3]`, step 2, bound 0x14; init state `[0xe6e6]=0xdfbc`,`[0xe6e8]=0xb6`; installers `c577`/`5f64`/`0f69:bddd`/`c99c`).  **The handlers are RUNTIME-installed by those init calls → NOT statically enumerable; enumeration/promotion is gated on reaching the dispatch, i.e. on the geometry.**
- **`FUN_0000_8329`** (per-object render) reads the object-type byte → type→method-index table at `DGROUP:[0x6d34]+0xb7` → `call cs:[si+0x8342]`.  cs-table 0x8342 = `{0x834a, 0x8358, 0x836e, 0x83c9, …}`. The task's "AX=0x700 = the voxel raycaster" conflates the two; corrected here.**

**PATCH 195 — the FUN_0000_4308 indirect-vector arg-scramble (the documented "generic `(*vec)()` positional-vs-varnode" hazard).**  4308 (patch 158) walks the mission model/unit table at `STRSEG(word[DGROUP:0x70]=0x2d74):0x802`, stride 8: `[+0]`=lod-flags, `[+2]`, `[+4]`=model-name index, `[+6]`=near vector; asm sets machine `CX=[+0], BX=[+2], AX=[+4]` and `call gs:[si+6]`. ## DONE (STAGE 3 — THE WHOLE 22dd MISSION-VIEWPORT PHASE NOW RUNS CRASH-FREE TO ITS TERMINATOR: the interpreter dispatches sub-list 1 end-to-end (`0x23ea` trig -> `0x41d0` op-0xc display-list record -> `0x3fba` crosshair -> `0x378e` object render-walk -> `0x41ee` -> `0x23ce` terminate), the loop EXITS (`word[0x450]=0`), and `459a` continues into the op-0x4c present pump. **EBX RECORD-RING QUESTION — RESOLVED (the flat-C model does NOT need to thread it for op-0xc): EBX is PROVABLY DEAD for op-0xc.**  The op-poster stubs do `mov gs:[si+0x3f2], EBX` (write the ambient carried register to the extender-TCB inbox at TCB+0x3f2).  The extender PM gate `FUN_00000f30` (`re_out/fist_image.bin`) does `ebx=[current-TCB+0x3f2]; call [op-table[op]]` -- BUT the op-0xc handler (op-table `[0xcb3+0xc]` = **`0x78f0`**) OVERWRITES ebx with `mov ebx,[0xc93]` (the current-TCB) as its FIRST instruction, before any use.  `0x78f0` then reads the RECORD fields (`[TCB+0x2]`/`[+0x4]` render-target seg:off, `[+0x1e]` width, `[+0x22]` height -- exactly what db99/ddff build) and renders the viewport.  So the posted EBX value is NEVER OBSERVED for op-0xc; `db99` (which never sets EBX) posts the carried garbage, which we write as 0 -- faithful (provably unread), NOT a fabricated value.  (EBX matters only for OTHER ops with different consumers, e.g. the op-0x4c present pump posts `EBX=&model[i]`, `0x26e4..0x2764` step 8 -- the model-seg array cursor; that consumer is `[0xcb3+0x4c]`=`0x123f` and is a separate frontier.)

**THE op-0xc EXTENDER CONSUMER — LOCATED + DECODED (Route-1, `re_out/fist_image.bin`), wiring deferred to a verifiable frame.**  op-table is 4-byte entries byte-indexed by op at image `0xcb3`; op 0xc -> `0x78f0`: `ebx=[0xc93]` (current-TCB); `ds:0x90ec = ((word[TCB+0x2]<<4)+word[TCB+0x4]+[0x807])` (render target), `ds:0x90f0 = word[TCB+0x1e]` (width), `ds:0x90f4=width>>1`, `ds:0x90f8 = word[TCB+0x22]` (height); then `call 0x85d0; call 0x93c0` (the viewport renderer).  NOT wired: `fist_extender_gate` logs op 0xc and returns 0, and `0x41d0` advances the cursor regardless (crash-free).  Wiring `0x78f0` awaits a verifiable frame -- the geometry it reads is still the uninitialised `0x7aa4`/`0x156a` viewport rect (the render is not bit-verifiable until the voxel raycaster + AE).

**PATCH 193 — promote + reconstruct `0x41d0 -> db99 -> ddff` (the op-0xc display-list geometry-record poster).**  `ddff` (base-loss fix, patch-186 class): builds the record at es:di=`word[0xea2e]:word[0xea2c]` (the DAT_2000_aa2c TCB macro) from the dst-geom `word[0x156a]` (near-offset, rebased) + src-geom `word[0x1552]`=0x7aa4.  `db99`: `call ddff`; copies the d548 struct `[0x12..0x1c]` + `word[0x7b1c]`/`word[0x7b18]>>2` + `word[word[0x156a]]` into the record; inbox=EBX (dead, written 0); op(`word[0xea10]`)=0xc; `call e339`.  **OBSERVED**: trap 0x041d0 -> 0x03fba; `[ext] service op 0x0c ... inbox=00000000` fires once; no SEGV.  asm-verified vs `re_out/fist_dat_image.bin`.

**PATCH 194 — complete sub-list 1 (`0x3fba` crosshair-draw + `0x40ec` erase, `0x41ee`->49b5, `0x23ce` terminate, `0x378e` object render-walk base-loss).**  The mission handler-offset table at `DGROUP:0x6c8e` (d549=0x1e seed) is `{0x23ea,0x41d0,0x3fba,0x378e,0x41ee,0x23ce}`.  0x3fba/0x40ec plot/restore a 0xff crosshair into the dst-geom off-screen page (viewport-bounds early-returns clip it out while the geom is uninitialised -- faithful, crash-free).  0x23ce terminates the loop (`word[0x450]=0`).  **0x378e (PRE-EXISTING, exposed by the 3fba advance)** = the mission-object RENDER-WALK: begin `far[0x6b0]`; loop { near iterator `[0x1630]` -> CF=done (via `g_fist_cf`) + updated si; dispatch `word[DGROUP: byte[0x6bc2] + 0x4b52]` (per-object render-method table) }.  Ghidra base-lost the dispatch (`*(u16*)(word[0x6bc2]+0x4b52)` wild deref -> SEGV) + folded the iterator CF; reconstructed instruction-faithful.  The iterator `[0x1630]` is RUNTIME-INSTALLED (OBSERVED: 2 objects walked, render methods `0x0c06d`/`0x0c33c` dispatched, then done -- so g_fist_cf is driven by the real iterator, not guessed).  **OBSERVED**: no SEGV/core; ALL 22dd handler traps (0x03fba/0x0378e/0x041ee/0x023ce/0x041d0) GONE; the phase runs to terminator, 459a continues.

**NEW FRONTIER — the PER-OBJECT RENDER DISPATCH (`0x378e`'s `word[DGROUP: byte[0x6bc2]+0x4b52]` method table).**  The object walk now dispatches each object's render method; those handlers (`0x0c06d`, `0x0c33c` seen this run) are unpromoted engine mid-entries that trap HARMLESSLY (no crash). ## DONE (STAGE 3 — THE FIRST 22dd MISSION-VIEWPORT HANDLER `0x23ea`/`0x23f2` IS PROMOTED + RECONSTRUCTED: the interpreter now runs the fixed-point trig/normalise handler to its cursor-advance and DISPATCHES THE NEXT HANDLER `0x41d0` (the trap moved FORWARD 0x023ea -> 0x041d0, observed).
